Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on heat structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 750. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 930 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the heat decomposition. By minimality, the element a^750 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 930-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a heat algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 285. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 111/4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on heat structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 285. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 111. QED.
